    public abstract class AssemblyDef : dnlib.DotNet.IAssembly, dnlib.DotNet.ICodedToken, dnlib.DotNet.IDnlibDef, dnlib.DotNet.IFullName, dnlib.DotNet.IHasCustomAttribute, dnlib.DotNet.IHasCustomDebugInformation, dnlib.DotNet.IHasDeclSecurity, dnlib.DotNet.IMDTokenProvider, dnlib.DotNet.ITypeDefFinder, dnlib.Utils.IListListener<dnlib.DotNet.ModuleDef>

        protected System.Version version;

        public string FullNameToken { get; }

        public System.Version Version { get; set; }

        public dnlib.DotNet.TypeDef? Find(string fullName, bool isReflectionName) { }

    [System.Flags]
    public enum FieldAttributes : ushort
    {
        FieldAccessMask = 7,
        PrivateScope = 0,
        CompilerControlled = 0,
        Private = 1,
        FamANDAssem = 2,
        Assembly = 3,
        Family = 4,
        FamORAssem = 5,
        Public = 6,
        Static = 16,
        InitOnly = 32,
        Literal = 64,
        NotSerialized = 128,
        SpecialName = 512,
        PinvokeImpl = 8192,
        RTSpecialName = 1024,
        HasFieldMarshal = 4096,
        HasDefault = 32768,
        HasFieldRVA = 256,
    }
    public abstract class FieldDef : dnlib.DotNet.ICodedToken, dnlib.DotNet.IDnlibDef, dnlib.DotNet.IField, dnlib.DotNet.IFullName, dnlib.DotNet.IHasConstant, dnlib.DotNet.IHasCustomAttribute, dnlib.DotNet.IHasCustomDebugInformation, dnlib.DotNet.IHasFieldMarshal, dnlib.DotNet.IIsTypeOrMethod, dnlib.DotNet.IMDTokenProvider, dnlib.DotNet.IMemberDef, dnlib.DotNet.IMemberForwarded, dnlib.DotNet.IMemberRef, dnlib.DotNet.IOwnerModule, dnlib.DotNet.ITokenOperand
    {
        protected int attributes;
        protected dnlib.DotNet.Constant? constant;
        protected bool constant_isInitialized;

        protected dnlib.DotNet.TypeDef? declaringType2;
        protected uint? fieldOffset;
        protected bool fieldOffset_isInitialized;
        protected dnlib.DotNet.ImplMap? implMap;
        protected bool implMap_isInitialized;
        protected byte[]? initialValue;
        protected bool initialValue_isInitialized;
        protected dnlib.DotNet.MarshalType? marshalType;
        protected bool marshalType_isInitialized;

        protected bool rva_isInitialized;
        protected dnlib.DotNet.CallingConventionSig? signature;

    [System.Flags]
    public enum MDStreamFlags : byte
    {
        BigStrings = 1,
        BigGUID = 2,
        BigBlob = 4,
        Padding = 8,
        DeltaOnly = 32,
        ExtraData = 64,
        HasDelete = 128,
    }
    [System.Diagnostics.DebuggerDisplay("DL:{dataReader.Length} R:{numRows} RS:{tableInfo.RowSize} C:{Count} {tableInfo.Na" +
        "me}")]
    public sealed class MDTable : System.IDisposable, dnlib.IO.IFileSection
    {
        public System.Collections.Generic.IList<dnlib.DotNet.MD.ColumnInfo> Columns { get; }
        public dnlib.IO.FileOffset EndOffset { get; }
        public bool IsEmpty { get; }
        public string Name { get; }
        public uint RowSize { get; }
        public uint Rows { get; }
        public dnlib.IO.FileOffset StartOffset { get; }
        public dnlib.DotNet.MD.Table Table { get; }
        public dnlib.DotNet.MD.TableInfo TableInfo { get; }
        public void Dispose() { }
        public bool IsInvalidRID(uint rid) { }
        public bool IsValidRID(uint rid) { }
    }

    {

    }

    }

        public void SetReadOnly() { }
        public override string ToString() { }
        public void WriteTo(dnlib.DotNet.Writer.DataWriter writer) { }
        protected abstract void WriteToImpl(dnlib.DotNet.Writer.DataWriter writer);
    }

namespace dnlib.PE
{
    [System.Flags]
    public enum Characteristics : ushort
    {
        RelocsStripped = 1,
        ExecutableImage = 2,
        LineNumsStripped = 4,
        LocalSymsStripped = 8,
        AggressiveWsTrim = 16,
        LargeAddressAware = 32,
        Reserved1 = 64,
        BytesReversedLo = 128,
        Bit32Machine = 256,
        DebugStripped = 512,
        RemovableRunFromSwap = 1024,
        NetRunFromSwap = 2048,
        System = 4096,
        Dll = 8192,
        UpSystemOnly = 16384,
        BytesReversedHi = 32768,
    }
    [System.Flags]
    public enum DllCharacteristics : ushort
    {
        Reserved1 = 1,
        Reserved2 = 2,
        Reserved3 = 4,
        Reserved4 = 8,
        Reserved5 = 16,
        HighEntropyVA = 32,
        DynamicBase = 64,
        ForceIntegrity = 128,
        NxCompat = 256,
        NoIsolation = 512,
        NoSeh = 1024,
        NoBind = 2048,
        AppContainer = 4096,
        WdmDriver = 8192,
        GuardCf = 16384,
        TerminalServerAware = 32768,
    }

    public interface IListListener<TListValue>
    {
        void OnAdd(int index, TListValue value);
        void OnClear();
        void OnLazyAdd(int index, ref TListValue value);
        void OnRemove(int index, TListValue value);
        void OnResize(int index);
    }
    [System.Diagnostics.DebuggerDisplay("Count = {Count}")]
    [System.Diagnostics.DebuggerTypeProxy(typeof(dnlib.Utils.CollectionDebugView<TValue>))]
    public class LazyList<TValue> : System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<TValue>, System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<TValue>, System.Collections.Generic.IList<TValue>, System.Collections.IEnumerable
        where TValue :  class
    {
        public LazyList() { }
        public LazyList(dnlib.Utils.IListListener<TValue>? listener) { }
        public int Count { get; }
        public bool IsReadOnly { get; }
        public TValue this[int index] { get; set; }
        public void Add(TValue item) { }
        public void Clear() { }
        public bool Contains(TValue item) { }
        public void CopyTo(TValue[] array, int arrayIndex) { }
        public dnlib.Utils.LazyList<TValue>.Enumerator GetEnumerator() { }
        public int IndexOf(TValue item) { }
        public void Insert(int index, TValue item) { }
        public bool Remove(TValue item) { }
        public void RemoveAt(int index) { }
        public struct Enumerator : System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerator<TValue>, System.Collections.IEnumerator, System.IDisposable
        {
            public TValue Current { get; }
            public void Dispose() { }
            public bool MoveNext() { }
        }
    }
    [System.Diagnostics.DebuggerDisplay("Count = {Count}")]
    [System.Diagnostics.DebuggerTypeProxy(typeof(dnlib.Utils.CollectionDebugView<TValue, TOther>))]
    public class LazyList<TValue, TContext> : dnlib.Utils.LazyList<TValue>, System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<TValue>, System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<TValue>, System.Collections.Generic.IList<TValue>, System.Collections.IEnumerable
        where TValue :  class
    {
        public LazyList() { }
